- **M16 step 5, Images section: Upscale (2026-11-15). Step 5 is done.**
  - *The trial (by hand first, outside DOG.ai; picture engine build `master-1104-5dcfa79`, an Illustrious checkpoint of 6.9 GB, 832 × 1216, 28 steps; memory is what the card held above the 1.8 GB Windows uses, in the units the app shows).* The engine can do three things to an existing picture. (1) **Redraw it larger from its seed** (the hires fix: draw, enlarge, refine). No extra file. Decoded whole, as every picture was until now: ×1.5 took 15.6 s and peaked at 16.1 GB; **×2 filled the card (23 GB), refined at one step a second and had not finished its last step after ten minutes**. Decoded in tiles (a switch in the request): ×1.5 19.9 s and 7.8 GB, **×2 25 s and 8.7 GB**, ×2.5 42 s and 10.0 GB, ×3 56 s and 11.4 GB. No seams were seen in the pictures looked at. It adds detail and changes small things (a rivet, the shape of an emblem). (2) **Enlarge it as it is with an upscaler file** (ESRGAN kind), through a route of its own: Real-ESRGAN x4plus, ×4, **8.5 to 10 s, half a gigabyte, and the checkpoint is not even read into memory**. Nothing in the picture changes; edges come out sharp, fine texture a little smoother. 832 × 1216 becomes 3328 × 4864 (0.9 to 1.3 MB as WebP). It takes a WebP and gives one back; it writes a small extra part into the file (how many times it enlarged, nothing else), which DOG.ai removes. The engine stops at 8192 pixels a side. The same file as the hires fix's enlarger: ×2 in 34 s, the same memory, not clearly better than the built-in one, so not offered. (3) **Redraw from the picture itself** (an input picture at low strength): ×2 in 16 s. It works, but it is image-to-image, which is not in, and such a picture could not be drawn again from its row. **File formats:** the engine reads `.safetensors`, `.pth` and `.gguf` upscalers, only from the top of the folder it is given, by name; a `.safetensors` Real-ESRGAN works (67 MB, Hugging Face, BSD 3-Clause; downloaded with the owner's yes). **It opens every file at the top of that folder each time it lists what it can do**, a `.pth` included. It ignores a waiting program going away: an enlargement runs to its end.
  - *What changed for the owner.* (1) **Upscale…** under the picture on the Make tab (between Use these settings and Save as a file…) and in a kept picture's large view (after Make one like this). It opens one dialog with the picture, its size now, and two ways. **Redraw larger**: "DOG.ai draws the picture again from its seed, with its own checkpoint and settings, enlarges it and refines the enlarged picture. It adds detail; small things can change." Sizes to choose: ×1.5, ×2 (chosen first), ×2.5, ×3 of the size the picture was first drawn at, each with its size in pixels; only sizes larger than the picture has, and none past 4096 pixels. A picture drawn with a hires fix keeps its own steps, strength and enlarger; any other gets the numbers Fine uses. **Enlarge only**: "An upscaler file makes the picture larger as it is. Nothing is drawn again, so nothing in it changes, and it takes little graphics memory." It lists the upscaler files at the top of the upscalers folder ("RealESRGAN_x4plus (×4)") and says what the picture becomes ("It becomes 3328 × 4864."). With none there it says so, with **Open the upscalers folder**, **Look again** and a link to Get models › Pictures. (2) **The result is a new picture beside the original; the original is never changed.** On the Make tab it is a new try in the strip ("Upscaled to 1664 × 2432 in 26 s. It is a new try in the strip, beside the one it came from; Keep keeps it."). For a kept picture it is shown in the dialog with its facts and **Keep it** or **Discard** ("It is in memory only until you keep it."); Keep it adds a new kept picture and opens it. (3) Progress reads "Upscale · Drawing, step 12 of 28…", "Enlarging the picture…", "Refining the enlarged picture, step 3 of 14…", or "Enlarging the picture with the upscaler file (about ten seconds)…". Cancel stops it; closing the dialog stops it too. It counts as a Draw: one at a time. (4) **An enlarged picture says so** in its facts ("3328 × 4864 · enlarged ×4 · seed …"); Make one like this on it gives the settings of the picture it came from. A redrawn one is an ordinary picture with a hires fix: Make one like this draws it again, byte for byte. (5) **When a way cannot be used, the dialog says why**: the picture's checkpoint, VAE or a LoRA is gone (a redraw needs them; Enlarge only does not, and is then chosen first); the enlarged picture would pass 8192 pixels; a `.pth`-style file sits in the upscalers folder ("Move old-style.pth out of the upscalers folder first…"). (6) **Real-ESRGAN x4plus is in Get models › Pictures** as a download (64 MB), and On this PC › Pictures lists an upscaler as usable ("Upscaler (ESRGAN kind, enlarges ×4). Upscale in Images uses it from the top of the upscalers folder."). (7) **The hires fix on the Make tab now works above ×1.5**: any picture whose largest pass is over 2.4 million pixels is decoded in tiles.
  - *Graphics memory.* The estimate knows the larger sizes: a picture decoded in tiles is judged at the model plus half a gigabyte per million pixels (×2: 10.1 GB judged, 8.7 measured; ×3: 12.6 judged, 11.4 measured), enlarging with an upscaler file at 1 GB. So with Huihui Qwen3.5 9B loaded, a ×2 redraw and an enlargement both left the chat model loaded (the log: "the picture needs about 10.1 GB and 5.9 GB is free (7.3 GB already held by the picture model), so the chat model stays loaded"), and a ×3 redraw set it aside (12.6 GB).
  - *Decisions taken here (say so to change one).* **Two ways, not three**: redraw from the seed and enlarge with a file; redrawing from the picture itself is left out because it is image-to-image. **Pictures up to Fine's size are drawn exactly as before** (decoded whole), so every kept picture still redraws byte for byte; only larger ones are decoded in tiles. **An enlarged picture keeps the settings of the picture it came from** and is told from its size, so the Pictures table needed no new column and no update of the database. **The picture engine is given the upscalers folder only while no `.pth`, `.pt`, `.ckpt` or `.bin` file is at its top**, because it opens every file there; with one there, Enlarge only is refused with the file's name, drawing goes on without the folder, and an engine already running with it is started again without it. Subfolders are not looked at by the engine, so a file there is not offered. **For a kept picture the result is shown before it is kept** (the plan said "kept as a new picture"): nothing the owner has not seen reaches the disk. **Enlarging uses the checkpoint already chosen** (the engine must be running, but the checkpoint is not loaded for it), else the picture's own, else any installed one.
  - *Found and fixed while building it.* A hires fix above ×1.5 on the Make tab (allowed since step 2) would have filled the card and hung, as in the trial: fixed by the tiles rule. A refusal from the engine with no code in it (the upscale route's kind) would have crashed instead of being said in words. After **Keep it** the dialog stayed open behind the new picture. On this PC › Pictures counted an upscaler as "not usable".
  - *How it is built.* Core/Images: `PictureUpscale.cs` (`UpscaleWay`, `UpscaleSource`, `RedrawSize`, `PictureUpscale`: `Redraw`, `RedrawSizes`, `EnlargedBy`, `EnlargeProblem`, wording), `ImagesSession.UpscaleAsync` and `IsUpscaling`, `ImageEnlarge` / `ImageEnlarged`, `IImageEngine.EnlargeAsync`, `CardCoordinator.EnlargeAsync` and `Enlarging`, `PictureMemory.Need` (tiles) and `EnlargeNeed`, `ImageRequest.LargestPass` / `DecodesInTiles` / `TiledAbovePixels`, `ImageModel.UpscalerFolder`, `WebpInfo.PictureOnly`, `ImageFileVerdict.Enlarges`, the download in `ImageDownloads`. Engine/Images: `SdApi.EnlargeAsync` / `EnlargeBody` / `UpscalersAsync` and the tiles switch in `Body`, `ImageServer.EnlargeAsync` and `--hires-upscalers-dir`, `ImageLibrary.Upscalers` / `UpscalerFolderProblem`. - **M16 step 4, Images section: Helpers (2026-11-15). Step 4 is done.**
  - *What changed for the owner.* Two new buttons beside the prompt boxes on the Make tab, after Saved prompts: **Write it for me…** and **Check my tags**. (1) **Write it for me…** opens a dialog: describe the picture in a sentence or two, press **Write it** (or Ctrl+Enter), and the chat model writes a prompt: tags for a tag checkpoint (every tag looked up in the tag list, so near misses are corrected and unknown words left out; the dialog says which), a short plain-words prompt for a checkpoint that reads sentences. It shows one line on what the picture shows and the prompt, with **Add after the prompt** (after what is typed, with a comma) and **Replace the prompt**; nothing reaches the box until one is pressed, and the negative prompt is never touched. It adds no quality, rating or LoRA words. It uses the chat model in memory, else loads the default for General ("Loading the chat model…"); a chat model set aside for pictures comes back first ("…set aside for pictures. It is loading again, then writes the prompt…"). Cancel stops it; closing the dialog stops it too; it cannot be used while a Draw is going on. The sentence stays while DOG.ai is open (also after closing the dialog), never after a restart. (2) **Check my tags** looks up every piece of both boxes in the tag list (the full list too when it was added) and lists, per box, what is not a tag: "pleted skirt → pleated skirt" with **Change**, or "frobnicate is not in the tag list" with **Take it out**, and **Leave it** for each; **Make every correction** when there are two or more. Nothing changes unless pressed, and then only that word, everywhere it is written: brackets and weights around it, line breaks and every other character stay as typed (in "(pleted skirt:1.2)" only the words change). Left alone: quality and rating words, the checkpoint's own quality words and negative prompt, the ticked LoRAs' trigger words, and what is not a plain tag (`<lora:…>`, BREAK, brackets inside brackets). A word whose describing part is unknown is not offered as its last word: "silvr hair is not in the tag list (only “hair” is)". Disabled for a checkpoint that reads sentences and when both boxes are empty. (3) **Trigger words as chips** under each ticked LoRA in the LoRAs fold ("+ icjournals"): a click puts the word after what is typed; a word already in the prompt shows "✓" and cannot be added twice. The words are the LoRA card's own (Models › On this PC › Pictures › LoRAs), else those found in the file; "No trigger words are known for it." when there are none. They are never added by themselves.
  - *Decisions taken here (say so to change one).* **The helper loads the default for General** when no chat model is in memory (the Images section has no default of its own). **The helper keeps to the rating in Settings › Pictures** (the model is told how far the picture may go, as the picture maker's planner is); no rating word is written into the prompt. **The helper asks the model how many people the description mentions**, and with none, code takes person tags out and starts with "no humans": before that, "a red fox in the snow" came back as "1girl, fox ears…" and "a cat on books" as a girl with a cat. **Words from a LoRA file are cleaned before they show as chips**, because they come from the file's maker: anything that looks like a path or a web address, longer than 60 characters, or on the tag list's left-out list is not shown (a line says how many, never which); at most 8. Words the owner typed on the LoRA's card show as typed; the file's own words stay cleaned even on a saved card (its editor starts filled with them, so a card saved only for its strength still holds them). **The tag check leaves a list of well-known quality and negative words alone** (masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, lowres, score_9 and the like, and the rating words of every family), so the boxes as they start are never offered a change.
  - *Found and fixed while checking.* Two wrong corrections in the tag list's rules, seen in the helper's output (they would also have reached portraits and story pictures): "center" became the rare tag "censer" (a short single word is no longer corrected into a tag with fewer than 1,000 posts), and "orange" became "orange (fruit)" (Danbooru files the bare word under the fruit; "orange" and "oranges" are no longer read as other spellings of it). Both have tests.

- **M16 step 3, Images section: Kept pictures (2026-11-15). Step 3 is done.**
  - *What changed for the owner.* (1) **Two tabs in the Images header, Make and Kept pictures** (centred in Workbench and Playhouse, under the title in Fireside). Ctrl+K has "Kept pictures"; Settings and Search go back to it ("Back to Kept pictures"). (2) **Kept pictures** is a grid of the pictures kept on the Make tab, newest first, 40 at a time with **Show more** and a line such as "420 kept pictures · showing 80". Portraits, story and chat pictures are not in it. Above the grid: **Find by words in the prompt** (every word, any case, part of a word counts; it looks 0.3 s after you stop typing), **Every checkpoint** (the checkpoints the kept pictures were drawn with) and **Every shape** (Tall, Square, Wide). With nothing kept it says "No kept pictures yet. Pictures you keep on the Make tab show here."; with filters that find nothing, "No kept picture matches." (3) **A picture opens large** with its facts (size, seed, sampler and steps, guidance, hires fix and LoRAs when used, checkpoint, when it was kept), its prompt and negative prompt, and **Make one like this** (its checkpoint, both prompts and every setting, seed included, go to the Make tab, which then draws the same picture), **Save as a file…**, **Remove** (asks first: "Yes, remove it" or "Keep it", with a line saying it leaves DOG.ai for good) and ‹ › to the newer and older picture. (7) The side panel holds one short note ("Kept inside DOG.ai, and locked with the rest when the lock is on. A picture leaves only through Save as a file."); below 1050 px it is the drawer, as everywhere.

- **M16 step 2, Images section: Make (2026-11-15). Step 2 is done.**
  - *What changed for the owner.* (1) **A new section, Images**, between Story and Models in all three looks (Workbench rail, Fireside and Playhouse top bars), on Playhouse's home screen ("Make pictures"; the fifth tile takes the whole last row) with its own amber colour, and in Ctrl+K ("Images", one of the main screens; Settings and Search go back to it as "Back to Images"). (2) **The Make tab** (layout B): the picture fills the page with the strip of tries beside it; under it a line of facts (try, size, seed, sampler and steps, time) and **Keep**, **Use these settings** and **Save as a file…**. The prompt and negative prompt boxes sit where the chat box is, with **Saved prompts**, the note "Sent exactly as typed." and **Draw** (it says "Draw 4" when four pictures are asked for), or **Cancel** while drawing. Progress reads "Picture 2 of 4 · Drawing, step 12 of 28…" and the picture so far shows in place. The checkpoint is chosen in the header; it does not change the Pictures default. (3) **The side panel** (a drawer below 1050 px) has seven folds: Model (VAE, and "Back to the card's numbers" once the sampling numbers were changed), LoRAs (tick and strength; their trigger words are not added), Size (Tall / Square / Wide and width and height), Sampling (sampler, scheduler, steps, guidance, clip skip), Seed (the M14 row: a number, Keep, New seed), Hires fix (on or off, enlarge by, steps, strength, Lanczos or Latent) and Pictures per Draw (1 to 8). Sampling and Hires fix start folded. Every control is remembered as soon as it is valid; a wrong value (a width that is not a multiple of 8, say) is said under the boxes and Draw waits. (4) **Several pictures per Draw** are drawn one after another into the strip; Cancel keeps the ones already drawn; leaving the page stops a Draw. With a seed set, the pictures take it, then the next numbers. (5) **Keep** puts the picture shown into DOG.ai (the Pictures table) with a small preview for step 3's grid; the try then says "kept" and has a green dot in the strip. **Use these settings** puts that picture's checkpoint, both prompts and every control back, seed included (so Draw gives the same picture until New seed is pressed). **Save as a file…** saves the WebP through the same save dialog as the exports (so the note shown while locked applies). (6) **Saved prompts** (a dialog from the button beside the boxes): **Save these as…** (a name; the same name replaces), and per saved prompt **Replace**, **Add** (after what is typed, with a comma) and a ⋯ menu with **Edit…**, **Start with this** (or **Don't start with this**) and **Delete…** (asks first).
  - *What is kept where.* The two boxes and the tries are **in memory only** (`ImagesSession`, a singleton): they survive going to another screen and back, and are gone after a restart or a lock. After a start the boxes begin with the saved prompt marked "Start with this", else the checkpoint's quality words and negative prompt, once per run (changing the checkpoint does not refill them). At most 32 tries are held (four Draws of eight); the oldest go first.
  - *Decisions taken here (say so to change one).* **`PictureTries` was not reused:** it applies a quality level to every request from a base at the card's step count (`At`), which would replace a typed size, the hires fix and the steps, and it loads the checkpoint with its card's VAE. The Make tab has its own small holder (`ImagesSession`, `ImagesTry`) that keeps each try exactly as sent (the one-picture request with the seed the engine used, checkpoint, VAE, prediction, time), so **Keep and Use these settings take what the picture was drawn with, never the side panel as it is now**. The side panel cannot be hidden on wide screens (like Models and Documents): the folds are plain `Fold`s with ids `images-model` … `images-count`, not chat panels. The Kept pictures tab is **not shown yet** (no tab that leads nowhere); its address `images/kept` is reserved as `ImagesPanels.KeptAddress` and is not in Ctrl+K. Save as a file was done here (it was small). The hires fix's size line says "about": the engine rounds the larger size up (832 × 1216 at ×1.5 came out 1280 × 1856, not 1248 × 1824).
  - *The preview (step 1's open item), now checked in the app.* The page makes it from the picture on a canvas (`pictures.js` `toPreview`, WebP at 0.82, 384 px on the longest side). WebView2's encoder wraps it in the extended WebP format with a colour profile (ICC, 456 bytes) beside the picture; nothing else (no EXIF, no XMP). `PicturePreview.Plain` (Core, with a test) keeps the picture part alone, so the preview stored is a plain lossy WebP: in the app, 263 × 384 and 9,564 bytes for an 832 × 1216 picture of 124,548 bytes, read back from the database's log file. A preview that fails `PicturePreview.Problem` is left out and the picture is kept anyway, with a note.
  - *How it is built.* Core/Images: `ImagesSession` (boxes, tries, `DrawAsync` of several pictures through `IImageEngine` one after another, `Cancel`, `Kept`), `ImagesTry` (`ToKeep`, `Src`), `ImagesPanels` (addresses, fold ids, one-line wording, the facts line, the progress line), `ImagesDraw.KeepAs`, `PicturePreview.Plain`. UI: `Pages/ImagesMake.razor` (`@page "/images"`), `Components/Images/SavedPromptsBox.razor`; `Modes` gained Images (navigation and Playhouse tile); `Icon` "images"; `--c-images` (amber, from the mock-up); `pictures.js` `toPreview`. `QuickJump.Screens` has Images. `CLAUDE.md` has the rules ("Images section: Make (M16 step 2)").

The pictures already drawn stay in the strip."); leaving the page 2.5 s into a Draw stopped it; changing the checkpoint changed the Model fold and kept the boxes; after a restart the controls were as left (checkpoint, count, seed, LoRA, hires fix) and the boxes began with the saved prompt marked Start with this, with no tries. Ctrl+K "images" lists Images first; Settings pressed twice from Images went back to it ("Back to Images"). **Never on disk:** on a fresh data folder, a marker word typed into the prompt and another into the negative prompt, a picture drawn and not kept, DOG.ai closed and started again: neither word is in any of the 195 files of the data folder (database, its log file, logs, WebView2 folder) or the models folder, nor in TEMP or the engine folder (files changed during the run), in UTF-8 or UTF-16, any case; the saved prompt's words were found in the database (so the search finds what is there). **All three looks, dark and light** at 1400 px: Make with two tries, the Saved prompts dialog, Playhouse's home (14 pictures, looked at on sheets of four). **The drawer at 700 and 1000 px** in all six (24 pictures, looked at on sheets; measured by script in each: drawer at the right edge, nothing wider than the window, focus on ✕, Escape closes it and puts the focus back on the drawer button). A Tall picture in a 700 × 563 window: the boxes start two lines and one line high, the picture keeps 220 px above the facts line and the page scrolls to the buttons. `D:\DOG.ai\Models` had 1,105 files and 68,979,361,007 bytes before and after; `D:\dogai-b` is empty again.
  - *Found and fixed while looking.* The seed row ran out of the side panel (the number now has its own line); the shape buttons repeated the sizes (now Tall / Square / Wide, the size in the tooltip); at 700 px the tries strip went under the picture and left it about 70 px tall (the strip now stays beside it down to 520 px); in a short window the picture box ran over the facts line; Playhouse's fifth home tile sat alone in half a row.
  - *Seen, not changed.* Two script input events sent in the same instant to the two boxes keep only the first (the second box's change is lost). Real typing (key presses) kept both boxes; a person cannot type into two boxes in one instant. Walkthroughs must pause between setting the two boxes.
  - *Not checked.* With a chat model loaded (the set-aside note after a Draw; the coordinator's waiting stages in the progress line) and with the lock on. The published portable build. Save as a file's dialog itself (the button calls the same `SaveFileAsync` as the exports). A VAE file chosen earlier and since removed ("(not found)"), and a ticked LoRA whose file is gone. Still open from step 1: the upgrade of a locked database in the app; search speed beyond 300 pictures. Still open from M15: a real download of a too-large model; Unload everything while a chat model is loading or during a benchmark.
- **M16 step 1, Images section: Core and storage (2026-11-15). Step 1 is done.** No screen yet; nothing the owner can see changed, except that kept pictures are now read faster.
  - *What it delivers for the later steps.* (1) **The raw request:** what is typed and set becomes exactly what the picture engine is sent. The two prompt boxes go out character for character (spaces, line breaks and all); no quality words, rating words or LoRA trigger words are added, and LoRAs go as files with strengths. Several pictures per Draw (1 to 8) are one request each, so each shows when it is done; with a seed set they take that seed, then the next number, and so on. (2) **The remembered controls:** the Images section's checkpoint and every control of the side panel (sampler, scheduler, steps, guidance, clip skip, VAE, size, LoRAs and strengths, seed, hires fix, pictures per Draw) in one document of their own. A control starts from the checkpoint's own settings card; the sampling numbers and VAE are remembered per checkpoint (a card differs from checkpoint to checkpoint) and go back to following the card when set equal to it; the rest is shared. **No prompt is in it**, and a test checks the document has no place for one. (3) **Saved prompts:** a name with a prompt and a negative prompt, stored as typed; the same name replaces; one can be marked "Start with this"; Replace and Add (after what is typed, with a comma, never twice). With none marked the boxes start with the chosen checkpoint's quality words and negative prompt. (4) **The Images owner kind:** any number of kept pictures, listed apart from portraits and passage pictures. (5) **Previews:** a small WebP (384 px on the longest side, 512 at most, the picture's own shape, no metadata) in the picture's own row, given when the picture is kept or later; never a file. (6) **Kept pictures a page at a time** (40 by default), newest first, with their previews and without the pictures themselves: found by words in the prompt (every word, any case, part of a word counts; the negative prompt is not searched), filtered by checkpoint and by shape (taller, square, wider), plus the list of checkpoints the filter can offer.
  - *Defaults chosen here (say so to change one).* The Images section has **a checkpoint of its own**, which starts as the Pictures default and does not change it (picking another checkpoint to try something should not change what portraits and story pictures are drawn with). **LoRAs start unticked**: a checkpoint's style LoRAs (which go into every portrait and story picture) are not ticked by themselves here, in keeping with "nothing is added". The size starts at Tall (832 × 1216); hires fix starts off with Fine's numbers ready; one picture per Draw; a new seed each time. The seed is remembered with the other controls.
  - *Found and fixed while building it.* (1) **Listing and searching kept pictures read every picture in full.** The database keeps a large value in a chain of pages after its row and walks the whole chain to reach any column behind it; the picture was the fourth column, with the prompt, sizes and everything else behind it. Measured with 300 pictures of 0.6 MB: finding a word took 212 ms and read 180 MB, a list of them 108 ms. The update (`PicturePreviews`) rebuilds the Pictures table once with the picture as the last column and the preview before it: the same search takes 1 ms, the list 1 ms. Ids go on counting where they were, so the id of a removed picture is never given out again. The database file keeps the room the old copy used (zeroed, reused by later pictures); with many kept pictures the first start after the update copies them once. (2) **A deleted or replaced saved prompt, and any replaced setting, now leaves the log file beside the database too.** A changed settings document was overwritten in the database but its old text stayed in the log file until DOG.ai closed or something was deleted. The tidy-up that follows a delete now also follows an overwrite of any settings document (this covers the chat prompt library and a removed Hugging Face token as well), and a replaced preview.
  - *How it is built.* Core/Images: `ImagesDraw` (`Request`, `Singles`, `Problems`, `Model`, `Keep`, `Like` for "Use these settings" and "Make one like this"), `ImagesControls` and `ImagesSampling`, `ImagesMemory` (document `images`), `SavedPicturePrompts` (document `picture-prompts`), `PictureOwners.Images`, `PicturePreview`, `PictureQuery` and `PicturePage`; `IPictureStore.PageAsync`, `CheckpointsAsync`, `PreviewAsync`, `SetPreviewAsync`. Data: migration `PicturePreviews` (the rebuild), `SqlitePictureStore`, `SqliteSettingsStore` telling `DatabaseTidy`. Both new documents are loaded at start. `CLAUDE.md` has the rules ("Images section: Core and storage (M16 step 1)").
  - *Checked.* `dotnet build`; `dotnet test` 1,061 pass (27 new: ImagesCoreTests, and additions to KeptPictureTests and RemovedDataTests). The tests cover: the request equal to what was typed, down to the text the engine is sent; every control carried; seeds for several pictures; problems in words; the controls remembered, per checkpoint and shared, a damaged document read as far as it makes sense, a settings save from an older copy not undoing them; saved prompts and the starting boxes; pages, search and filters against a real database; previews accepted and refused. **Lock:** a database locked from its first day holds an Images picture with its preview, a preview given later and replaced, a saved prompt deleted and saved again, and the controls, with none of it readable in any file at any moment; encrypting a plain database hides the preview. **Backup and restore**, all four cases (plain or locked backup into a plain or locked DOG.ai): the picture, its preview, the saved prompt with its mark and the controls come back. **Portable:** a moved folder rewrites only the settings row; the picture's row, preview included, is byte for byte the same and the controls read the same. **Removed data**, in a plain database while DOG.ai runs: a removed Images picture takes its preview and both prompts off the disk; a replaced preview and a deleted or replaced saved prompt are gone from every file. **Upgrade:** a database from before, with a portrait, a passage picture and a removed one, comes through with every column and byte the same, the picture last, ids continuing, and a deleted character still taking its pictures along. **In the app, a real upgrade:** the previous build (f010ff7) drew and kept a real test picture (832 × 1216, 130 KB, seed 3039810870, one Illustrious checkpoint through a hard link in `D:\dogai-b`, the owner's picture engine); this build then started on the same data folder, showed the same picture (the page's copy identical, 178,503 characters), and "Draw again and compare" said "identical to the kept one". In the database file afterwards the prompt's marker word and a piece of the picture each appear once (the old table's copy is not left behind) and no `Pictures_new` remains. `D:\DOG.ai\Models` had 1,105 files and 68,979,361,007 bytes before and after; `D:\dogai-b` is empty again.
  - *A test that failed now and then, fixed.* The removed-data tests' first check ("it is on disk before it is removed") failed once in this session too, as noted under M13 and piece A. The cause: the tidy-up after start moved pages from the log file into the database between the test's reading of the one file and the other, so one look could miss what was there. The tests now look twice (which makes the "it is gone" checks stricter, not looser); three full runs in a row passed afterwards.
  - *Not checked.* A preview made by the page (there is no screen; the rules were tested with WebP headers made in the tests, so step 3 must check that what the page makes is accepted: lossy WebP, no metadata, at most 512 px and 256 KB). The upgrade of a locked database in the app (covered by tests only), and of a database with many large pictures (the 300-picture measurement was a test database). How long the search stays fast with thousands of pictures was not measured beyond 300. The published portable build (nothing portable-specific changed). No leak check with marker words in the app: nothing draws from the Images section yet (step 6 has it).
- **M15, Graphics memory and downloads (2026-11-15). M15 is done.**
  - *What changed for the owner.* (1) **Unload everything** under "Graphics memory" in the Models side panel (every Models tab that shows graphics memory, and in the drawer below 1050 px). It takes the chat model, the picture model, the document search model and a speech recognition model on the graphics card out of memory, then says what went ("Unloaded the chat model and the picture model.") and the readout is measured again. The chat model is unloaded as your own unload: no page loads one again until you pick a model. The checkpoint stays chosen, so the next picture loads the picture model by itself; the search and speech models come back when next needed. While a reply is being written, a picture is being drawn or a benchmark runs, the button is disabled and the line under it says why; it turns itself on again when that ends. A search model that is indexing documents is left alone and the note says so ("…is being used, so it stays"). A new line above the button says what is in memory now. (2) **The document search model leaves memory after two minutes unused** (was ten), and **at once when the card needs the room**: before a picture sets the chat model aside, before a chat model is loaded with part of it in system memory (or refused), before a benchmark, and when a chat model that was set aside comes back and would not fit. It is never stopped while a search is running or documents are being indexed. A speech recognition model on the graphics card makes way the same way (an addition: it holds about 0.6 GB and comes back with the next thing said). The Documents side panel now reads "In memory", "In memory, indexing documents", "Loading…" or "Not in memory (loads when needed)". (3) **Any model can be downloaded.** Download is no longer disabled for a model judged "Too large for this PC" (Recommended, Browse). The red "Too large for this PC" stays beside the button, and one confirmation names the size and why it will not run here, with **Cancel** and **Download anyway**. On Recommended, a model with no version that can run lists its smallest version, marked too large, instead of no file. Loading such a model is still refused. (4) **"Fits beside pictures"** mark (green, with an ⓘ) on chat models that can stay in memory while a picture is drawn: Recommended, a model's files in Browse, and On this PC › Chat models. "How fit is judged" has a line naming the checkpoint and the memory a Standard picture needs. No mark without a picture engine or a checkpoint.
  - *What the real numbers changed.* The size estimate for a chat model errs high by 1 to 2.3 GB on this PC (Huihui Qwen3.5 9B Q8_0 at 8,192: judged 10.6 GB, really 8.3 GB; a 7.1 GB Q6_K: judged 10.4, really 9.3; the 27B Q4_K_M: really 17.0 GB). By estimate alone Huihui would not be marked, yet it stays loaded beside a picture. So a model's real graphics memory is now measured two seconds after it loads whole on the card and kept in its record (two new columns, migration `ModelMemory`); the mark uses it for that context size, and the estimate until the model has been loaded once. Recommended and Browse know only the estimate, so they mark fewer models than really fit (Qwen3.5 4B there; the 9B only once installed and loaded). The mark is the card coordinator's own rule asked in advance, so it moves with what other programs use: Huihui clears the line by about 0.3 GB.
  - *How it is built.* `ICardGuest` (Core/Images: name, holds memory, busy, let go) is what else sits on the card; `EmbeddingServer` and `WhisperRecognizer` are guests of `CardCoordinator`. `CardCoordinator.ClearAsync` (Unload everything, `CardCleared.Text`), `MakeWayAsync` (idle guests go), `InMemory`, `BusyReason`; `ModelLauncher.UnloadEverythingAsync` and `MakeWayAsync`; `LoadDecision.WantsRoom`. `EmbeddingServer.IdleStop` (2 minutes), `KeepWhile` (indexing), `LetGoAsync`. `BesidePictures` and `TooLargeDownload` (Core/Models); `ModelRecord.MeasuredVramBytes` / `MeasuredAt`; `ModelsContext.FitsBesidePictures`, `PictureNeed`; `BesidePicturesChip`. Fit badges now count what the picture model holds as free, since loading a chat model lets it go. `CLAUDE.md` has the rules ("Graphics memory, the search model and downloads (M15)").
  - *Checked.* `dotnet build`; `dotnet test` 1,034 pass (23 new, GraphicsMemoryTests: Unload everything and its wording, refused during a reply or a picture, an indexing search model left, guests making way for a picture and a returning chat model, the mark's rule against the coordinator's, measured memory replacing the estimate and kept in the database, the confirmation's wording, the screens' ids). **In the app, on real models** (Debug build; throwaway data and models folders in `D:\dogai-b` with hard links to Huihui Qwen3.5 9B Q8_0, a 7.1 GB Q6_K, the 27B Q4_K_M, Qwen3 Embedding 0.6B, one Illustrious checkpoint and Whisper large-v3 turbo; the owner's picture engine through `DOGAI_SD_SERVER`): *Unload everything* with a chat model and the picture model in memory: 17.9 GB in use before, 2.3 GB after (an independent nvidia-smi trace: 17,888 MiB to 1,961 MiB), no engine program left, and opening General afterwards loaded nothing; again with chat, search and speech models together: 12.6 GB to 2.3 GB, all three named; disabled with "A picture is being drawn…" during a test picture and "A reply is being written…" during a reply, and enabled again by itself 25 s later when the reply ended; pressed while 3,034 passages were being indexed: "Nothing else was loaded. The document search model is being used, so it stays." *Search model:* in memory after a search, still in at 100 s, out at 140 s; let go with the speech model when a picture needed the room (the chat model stayed); let go when the 27B was loaded at 16,384 tokens and did not fit beside it; the Documents panel showed each state. *The mark, in a character chat:* with Huihui (marked once loaded) a reply was written, Illustrate and Draw made a picture, and the chat model stayed loaded ("13.0 GB is free… so the chat model stays loaded", llama-server still running); with the 27B (unmarked) the same steps set it aside ("only 4.7 GB is free… the chat model (17.2 GB) was set aside"). The 7.1 GB Q6_K, unmarked, was set aside too. *Downloads* (online for file lists only; nothing was downloaded, the downloads list stayed empty and the models folder had the same six files): at 131,072 tokens two Recommended picks became too large and listed their smallest version with Download enabled; the confirmation read "…is a 9.4 GB download. With a context of 131,072 tokens it needs about 34.1 GB of memory, and this PC has about 34.0 GB… so DOG.ai will not load it here."; Cancel and Escape both closed it. In Browse, a 70B model's 19 too-large files each had Download enabled beside "Too large for this PC", and the 131 GB one asked the same way. **All three looks, dark and light:** Chat models with the mark and its ⓘ open, the side panel after Unload everything, Recommended with the mark, Browse with too-large files, the confirmation, Documents with the panel's ⓘ open (36 pictures at 1400 px, every one looked at on sheets of four). **The drawer at 700 and 1000 px** in all six: Models (button, note, fit line) and Documents (60 pictures; twelve looked at, on three of the sixteen sheets: Fireside dark and Workbench at 700 px, Playhouse at 1000 px; every one measured by script: nothing wider than the window, the drawer at the right edge, focus on ✕, Escape closes it). `D:\DOG.ai\Models` had 1,105 files and 68,979,361,007 bytes before and after; `D:\dogai-b` is empty again.
  - *Not checked.* A real download of a too-large model (not wanted; the button runs the same download as any other once confirmed). Unload everything while a chat model is still loading, and during a benchmark (the wording is in the page; the benchmark case is not covered by a test). The speech model making way was seen once (in the log, with the search model); what Windows frees when it goes was not measured by itself. The mark on a PC with a smaller card, or with no picture engine (tests only). The published portable build (nothing portable-specific changed; the new columns are in `ModelRecords`, which a portable move already rewrites). **Seen, not changed:** the fit estimate's 1 to 2.3 GB overshoot also decides how a model loads, so a model near the limit can be loaded partly on the card when it would fit whole (the 27B at 16,384 was judged at 21.6 GB). Worth its own look.
- **M14, Quick fixes (2026-11-15). M14 is done.**
  - *What changed for the owner.* (1) **A seed row in Edit and redraw** (the picture maker): the seed as a number you can change, **Keep** (the default: the same seed as before, so nothing changes unless asked) and **New seed** (one is picked when it is drawn). A typed number is drawn exactly. Anything that is not a whole number from 0 to 4,294,967,295 says so under the row and Redraw waits until it is fixed. Every redraw is a new try; the picture it came from, a kept portrait included, is not touched until Use. The help line now reads "Change what you like and draw it again as a new try. Keep the seed to keep the picture close." (2) **Settings and Search go back.** Clicking Settings while in Settings (any category), or Search while in Search, returns to the screen you were on (a chat, a group chat, a story, an editor, a Models tab…); the button's tooltip then says "Back to the chat", "Back to Models › On this PC › Chat models" and so on. With nothing remembered it goes to the look's start page (Playhouse: Home). From Settings, Search still goes into Search, and back from there goes to where you were. Escape and Ctrl+K work as before. (3) **Unsaved typing in the character editor and story setup is kept** while you are on another screen (owner's decision, 2026-11-15, after it turned out that leaving an editor lost the typing with no warning; the plan had assumed a warning). Coming back shows it with "Your unsaved changes are back. They are kept only while DOG.ai is open; Save to keep them." and **Discard them**. Save and Cancel forget it. It lives in memory only: never on disk, gone after a restart or a lock. It is held whenever the editor is left, not only through the Settings button (it is done when the page closes); in the app it was seen with the Settings and Search buttons.
  - *How it is built.* `SeedChoice` (Core/Images: Keep, New, a typed number with a problem in words) and `PictureTries.RedrawAsync(..., seed)`; the row is `Components/Images/SeedRow.razor`, shared for M16 (`Id`, `Original`, `@bind-Value`; ids `{Id}-number`, `-keep`, `-new`, `-note`; the maker uses `maker-seed`). `ReturnPlace` (Core/Search) remembers the last address that is neither Settings nor Search (fed by `MainLayout`, including the first address) and names it from `QuickJump.Screens`; the shells' buttons are `Layout/PlaceButton.razor` (`#nav-settings`, `#nav-search`), a plain link with its own `active` class. `UnsavedForms` (Core/Settings) holds a form per address and fingerprints forms (`Print`, pictures by hash) to tell whether anything changed; `CharacterEditor` and `StoryEditor` hold on leaving and take back on opening. `tools/walkthrough.ps1` has a new step, `@{ type = '...' }` (real typing into the focused box).
  - *Checked.* `dotnet build`; `dotnet test` 1,011 pass (QuickFixTests: seed reading, Keep/New/typed redraws and a refused one sending nothing, a kept picture redrawn with a new seed staying kept, back targets and names for every screen, the shells using the button, held forms and fingerprints). **In the app** (Debug build, throwaway data folders; for pictures a throwaway models folder on D: with hard links to Huihui Qwen3.5 9B and one Illustrious checkpoint, removed afterwards; `D:\DOG.ai\Models` had 1,105 files and the same total size before and after): four real pictures drawn: Keep showed and used the try's seed, "x" typed gave the note and a disabled Redraw, New seed drew with a new seed (186155878), 12345 typed drew with seed 12345, then Keep at Draft kept 12345; Use, crop and Save kept the portrait, and reopening the maker showed "The picture in use" with its seed in the row. Back from Settings (real mouse clicks on the buttons) into a character chat, a group chat, a story, Models › On this PC › Chat models, General, a character page, the character editor and story setup; back from Search; Search ↔ Settings; `settings/voice` back; Escape in Settings did nothing; Ctrl+K opened there; Playhouse's home ("Back to Home"). Unsaved typing in the character editor (name and description, on the Personality tab) came back with its tab after Settings and after Search; Discard restored the saved card; Save then left nothing held; an untouched editor showed no note; Cancel forgot the typing; a new character and story setup kept theirs too. **All three looks, dark and light:** Settings page with the button active, the editor with the note, the seed row with Keep and with the note (24 pictures, looked at on contact sheets).
  - *Not checked.* The seed row and the note in a narrow window. Unsaved typing in the lore editor, personas and Assistants and prompts (not held; only the two editors named in the plan), and the chat box's unsent text (lost on leaving, as before). - **The checks that need a download, and the leftover checks (2026-11-15, after M13; follow-up piece B). Done.**
  - *Downloads made* (each asked for first; everything in the throwaway folder `D:\dogai-b`, a portable copy of this build; `D:\DOG.ai` only read, and its file count and size were the same before and after): Whisper base, English only (60 MB, Hugging Face); the chat engine, llama.cpp b12359 CUDA 13.5 (606 MB, GitHub); the picture engine, stable-diffusion.cpp master-1104 CUDA 12 (976 MB, GitHub). Each once, as the owner chose.
  - *What was checked in the app, and works.* (1) **A failed download**: "Failed" on the Downloads button in the Models header, "Failed" on the Voice row, the reason in the list with Retry and Cancel; it stays failed after a restart. (2) **A voice download finishing while a chat is open**: Retry, then straight to General: the composer showed without the microphone, and the microphone and hands-free buttons appeared about a second later, without leaving the page; DOG.ai went offline by itself when it finished. (3) **Engines**: the picture engine installed for real while the chat engine's Install button was disabled; then the chat engine installed for real while the picture engine's Install again was disabled, and the chat engine install moved the picture engine across into the new engine folder (it still read as installed, and both programs start). Then, with both marked as older builds and no second download (the picture engine's two downloaded files were kept and put back; the chat engine's were not kept in time, so its Update used two archives rebuilt from the installed copy, same names and contents): **Update** of the chat engine with a model loaded (the note "Installing unloads the current model first" showed, the model was unloaded, the picture engine came across again), **Update** of the picture engine, and **Install again**. Each ended "Up to date" with the version files right.
  - *Fixed.* (1) **A refused write** now says so in words: "Windows did not let DOG.ai write the file in <folder>. Check that the folder is not read-only and that nothing else is using the file, then Retry." (was Windows' "Access to the path '…\x.bin.part' is denied."; `DownloadManager.FailureText`, a test). (2) **Escape did not close a dialog** unless the focus was already inside it, and nothing put it there, so after opening a dialog with a button (Edit styles…, What the model was sent, Branch map, every `Modal`) Escape did nothing until a click inside. - **Thinking in chats (2026-11-15, after M13; piece B of the follow-ups). Done.**
  - *What changed for the owner.* (1) **Thinking switches for chats** in Settings › Chats and stories, under a new heading "Thinking": **Let the model think in General** (on by default) and **Let the model think in character and group chats** (off by default), beside the existing one for stories (off). (2) **A reply with no answer says why**, in place of the empty bubble: "No answer: the model's thinking (4,050 tokens) filled all the room it had (4,096 tokens, the context size), so it could not write one", then what to do, with links: turn off the switch for that kind of chat, or set a larger context size in Models › On this PC › Chat models, then Regenerate. A model that stopped by itself after thinking says so (no room advice); a reply stopped with Stop while thinking says "Stopped while the model was still thinking". Replies written before this change get the note too. Stories show the same note on a passage, pointing at "Let the model think in stories" and Rewrite. (3) **Nothing follows a reply with no answer:** in a group chat the round ends there (the next member is not asked), and an adventure passage with no text gets no choices. (4) **The character editor's draft box never thinks** (the same fault M13 fixed for summaries: a 3,000-token limit a thinking model could spend on thinking).
  - *How it is built.* `ChatOptions` (Core/Chat; like `StoryOptions`; `AppSettings.GeneralThinking`, default true, and `RoleplayThinking`), applied in `ChatService.ThinkingFor` for replies and drafts. `ThinkingOnly` (Core/Chat) tells the case from what the reply saved: speed figures arrive only when the model ends by itself, so a reply without them was stopped; prompt plus reply ending within 64 tokens under one of the context sizes DOG.ai offers (now one list, `ContextSizes` in Core/Models) ran out of room at that size, so the note stays right after the owner raises the size. `NoAnswerNote` (UI, Components/Chat) shows it, in `MessageView`'s new `Instead` slot and in `StoryThread`. `GenerateAsync` returns null for a new reply with no text (it is still saved).

Arrow keys choose, Enter opens, Escape or a click outside closes and puts the typing back where it was; Ctrl+K again closes it too. Before anything is typed it lists the five chats used last, then the main screens. A setting opens its category and flashes. Names come first: a character before chats named after them, then the chats with them. It does not open over another dialog (the picture maker, a confirmation). The Search screen has a **Jump to… Ctrl+K** button for the mouse, and the Search button's tooltip names the key. Because it lists every setting, a setting can now be found by name in a narrow window too, where the Settings side list and its search box are hidden. (2) **Shorter help text on the screens M10 to M12 had not touched** (23 texts in 18 files): Models › Engines (both install notes), Get models › Recommended, Pictures and Voice, On this PC › Pictures (the checkpoint note, a checkpoint's rating words, the extra tags note) and Voice, the Connection panel, the model picker's context size, the document search model panel, the lore editor, the story editor (the draft box, character cards, the outline), the character editor's draft box, the Looks box and the picture maker. One line stays; the rest is behind the small ⓘ button. Requirements and warnings stay visible ("It needs an NVIDIA graphics card", "DOG.ai never goes to Civitai"). On the Documents screen "How it works" folds away (closed until opened, remembered) and says "Everything stays on this PC" while closed. (3) **Faults found during the final pass and fixed.** The **⋯ menu on the last model card** opened downwards and was cut off, so Delete could not be reached; a menu with no room below now opens upwards. **A chat with a model that thinks first never got a memory summary:** the summary was asked for with thinking allowed and a limit of 1,024 tokens, which such a model spends on thinking; summaries are now always asked for without thinking (stories too, whatever "Let the model think in stories" says). **Models said "Too large for this PC" about every model** for the first seconds after the section was first opened, while the PC was still being measured (sometimes ten seconds); those tabs now say "Measuring this PC…" until it is. **Playhouse's home screen always said "No model is loaded yet"**, even with one loaded; it now says which model is ready or loading. The old addresses open their new places and `models/downloads` opens the downloads list. **A test picture was drawn** from the folded section (Draft, 22 s, on a throwaway models folder holding a hard link, removed afterwards); Engines then said the picture engine was running; the fold stayed open after leaving the page. **A real download** (owner agreed; Piper: Joe, 77 MB, into a temporary folder, removed afterwards): Go online, the count while running, Pause ("Paused"), Escape closing the list, the paused voice not listed under On this PC, Resume, finished ("Installed" in Get models, listed under On this PC › Voice), Go offline. Found and fixed while looking: Fireside's downloads list opened over the Models list and ran off the window at 700 px (now centred under its button; after the fix its position was measured at 700 and 1400 px, not looked at again); the "picture engine is not installed" note showed on Tags, where it means nothing.
